NOTIFICATIONS BY GOVERNMENT

LAW DEPARTMENT
LIST OF NOTARIES APPOINTED BY GOVERNMENT OF TAMIL NADU AND
IN PRACTICE AT THE BEGINNING OF THE YEAR 2025
(See rule 17)
[G.O. Ms. No. 22, Law (Administration), 31st January 2025, Thai 18, Kurothi, Thiruvalluvar Aandu-2056.]
No. II(2)/LAW/82(a)/2025.
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 6 of the Notaries Act, 1952 (Central Act 53 of 1952),
Governor of Tamil Nadu hereby publishes the following list of Notaries appointed by the Government of
Tamil Nadu and in practice at the beginning of the year 2025.
